# Skills · A · part 188

845 entries, audio-and-metasounds to audit. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **audio-and-metasounds**: The Unreal audio pipeline: a **sound asset** (`USoundBase` subclass) is activated either as a fire-and-forget one-shot or via a **`UAudioComponent`** for ongoing control. 3D sounds are positioned and faded using a **`USoundAttenuation`** asset. Mixing is handled through **sound classes**, **submixes**, and **concurrency** objects. MetaSounds are the modern, node-based, parameter-driven replacement ([ziyarduc/Baran-bk-game](https://github.com/ziyarduc/Baran-bk-game/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/audio-and-metasounds) · ★1)
- **audio-and-signal**: Playbook for Kaggle audio and biosignal competitions — bioacoustics (BirdCLEF-style), speech, EEG/ECG/EMG, seismic and other 1D signals — covering spectrogram vs raw-waveform models, augmentation, weak labels, soundscape inference under CPU limits, and subject-grouped validation. Use for any audio or sensor-waveform data. ([TranBaDat2607/claude-code-kaggle-grandmaster](https://github.com/TranBaDat2607/claude-code-kaggle-grandmaster/tree/HEAD/plugins/kaggle-grandmaster/skills/audio-and-signal) · ★2)
